{"parentUuid":"de57f5b7-f6bf-45f2-ac82-dedba125fbdb","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfk1Lswgpd7rmtYJ1Srbt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"T05 is committed as `dcdd9b6` with `Closes #6`, and the full suite passes (98 tests). mypy still reports one error, in `scripts/check_submission.py`, and it was there before my changes.\n\nWhat it does:\n- **Locked flag:** pages now have one. It is added to existing databases, and `seed_demo.py` now creates the five pages with the Team Schedule Locked. It also locks an existing Team Schedule if the database was made before T05.\n- **Editor on a Locked page:** an editor can open and read it, but save, publish, unpublish and delete are refused with a 403 refusal page. The form fields are read-only for them.\n- **Lock and unlock:** only an admin sees and can use these. A forged request from an editor is refused.\n- **Refusal page:** it is reachable at `/admin/refused` for screenshots, and the wording matches the cause.\n- **Access-control tests:** `tests/test_t05_locked.py` builds the admin-only route list from the app's router. It asserts editors get 403 or a redirect, and anonymous visitors are redirected to `/login`.\n- **Existing test updated:** I added the lock and unlock routes to the CSRF guard test in `tests/test_t02_pages.py`.\n\nI posted the code-review findings and how I resolved each on issue #6. I left two things alone:\n- **Refactors for T06:** the repeated role checks, the duplicated lock/unlock forms and routes, and moving the refusal helper out of `main.py`.\n- **A gap in the route test for T06:** a new admin-only route that doesn't use `require_admin` would not be picked up by the access-control test.\n\nI left `notes/usage-ledger.csv` and the transcript files uncommitted. They were already modified or untracked before this session. {"parentUuid":"fd9266fb-6cc2-41dd-9168-6c12fc80cf5a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011Cfk1TkH1jQBuYwUuGzrRq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"No, it isn't a real problem. It's a typing nit in a helper script and doesn't affect the app or T05.\n\nThe flagged line is `self.add(transcripts and not bad, ...)`. Here `transcripts` is a list, so `transcripts and not bad` evaluates to either `[]` or a bool. mypy objects because `add()` is typed `ok: bool`. At runtime it's fine, because `add()` wraps the value in `bool(ok)` before storing it. An empty list becomes `False` and a non-empty list with no bad names becomes `True`, which is the intended behaviour.\n\nThe error is in `scripts/check_submission.py`, which came in with the initial commit. It's a submission-checking script, not part of the CMS. Nothing in `app/` is affected.\n\nThe fix is a one-liner: change that argument to `bool(transcripts) and not bad`. I can make it as a separate small commit if you want mypy to run clean. I left it out of the T05 commit because it's unrelated."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1071,"cache_read_input_tokens":87706,"output_tokens":326,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1071,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":326,"cache_read_input_tokens":87706,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1071,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1071},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"serverClassifierRequest":"1de9a8bb-edb8-46e1-af16-41edb324851c","apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011Cfk1Tjz9szrZJEcCqY8tt","type":"assistant","uuid":"3cb6971e-ce8c-4497-8ce9-b4d44d55d7f7","timestamp":"2026-10-06T00:07:05.227Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"9d3ee875-b856-4c5f-8263-d5e667d38523","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/sphod/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"6ed77c51-fd1c-46b7-a91f-cda8390f3bd3","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
